- Given binary h with n/2 ones, compute correlations counts C[l] for lags -n+1..n-1.

We can compute multiset:

Difference = remove pairs involving i as in A_o with s∈B_o: {(i,s): s∈B_o}; remove pairs involving j as in B_o with r∈A_o: {(r,j): r∈A_o}; add pairs involving j as in A_n with s∈B_n: {(j,s): s∈B_n}; add pairs involving i as in B_n with r∈A_n: {(r,i): r∈A_n}.
Now B_o includes j, not i. A_o includes i, not j. B_n includes i, not j. A_n includes j, not i.

Let's design binary local search in detail.

```
def binary_local_search(h0,n,deadline,max_time,seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = top_n_binary(h0,n)
    if n%2==0 and sum != n/2 maybe adjust.
    c = np.array(np.correlate(h, 1-h,'full'), int32)
    M = int(c.max())
    besth=h.copy(); bestM=M
    target? not needed, we minimize M.
    D=build_D(h,n)
    ones=zeros...
    it=0
    last_improve=time.time()
    # maybe initial random shuffle for scan.
    while time.time() < deadline:
       # scan for best move with newmax < M (or newmax <= M if plateau)
       # To save time, do full scan if M > target? We'll pass maybe target_T.
       best_new = M+1
       best_ii=best_jj=-1
       # We can scan pairs in chunks. Use a queue of pair blocks randomized each iteration? For deterministic, compute arrays:
       ones_idx = np.flatnonzero(h==1)
       zeros_idx = np.flatnonzero(h==0)
       # randomize order of zeros (or pairs) to avoid bias; use rng.permutation(zeros_idx) maybe cost O(n^2) not; just random chunk order.
       # All pairs = n^2/4; chunk size determined.
       chunk = 512 if n<=300 else 256
       # Preallocate? Ddiff each chunk.
       for sz in range(0, zeros_idx.size, chunk):
          j_chunk = zeros_idx[sz:sz+chunk]
          # Pair with all ones maybe chunk * ones.size could be 512*200=102k, Ddiff 102k*400? too big. Need chunk over pairs, not zeros.
```

```
def lse_objective_grad(h,n,beta,pen):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  dx=2/n
  v=1-h
  c=np.correlate(h,v,'full')*dx
  c=np.maximum(c,0)
  m=c.max()
  y=beta*(c-m)
  e=np.exp(np.minimum(y,0))
  p=e/e.sum()
  F=m+np.log(e.sum())/beta
  P=p
  R=P[::-1]
  # Need gradient with respect to h: c_k = ∑ h[r] v[r-k]. d/dc? P weights. g_i = ∑_k P_k [ (1-h_{i-k}) - h_{i+k} + (k=0? -2h_i?)? Wait c0 derivative (1-2h_i) but weighted. For k≠0: for h_i as first factor: (1-h_{i-k}) (if valid); as second factor in h_{i+k}(1-h_i): -h_{i+k}. So B_i = (i-k valid?1-h_{i-k}:0) - (i+k valid?h_{i+k}:0). This equals our D? D[p]=H[p+d]-Z[p-d]. Our B? D? For k=d, B_d,i includes -? We can compute via convolutions as previous:
  A = np.convolve(R, v, mode='full')[n-1:2n-1]
  B = np.convolve(P, h, mode='full')[n-1:2n-1]
  g = dx*(A - B)
```
Let's verify: Previous code used P weights, R reverse, A=conv(R,v)[...], B=conv(P,h)[...], g=dx(A-B). Does it include k=0? It should. For p weights, ∂/∂h_i ∑_k P_k c_k. c_k = ∑_r h_r (1-h_{r-k}). For fixed i, contributions:
- as second factor h_{r-k}=h_i => r=i+k: term P_k * h_{i+k} * (-1) => -∑_k P_k h_{i+k}.
- as first factor h_i: term P_k*(1-h_{i-k}) => ∑ P_k (1-h_{i-k}).
So g_i = ∑_k P_k(1-h_{i-k}) - ∑_k P_k h_{i+k}. Their conv: A=conv(R,v)[...] where R[k]=P_{-k}? Need but dx(A-B) likely correct. We trust previous.
```
  return F, g
```
But if c has zero terms? Correlations nonnegative? For fractional, c can be negative? h(1-h) terms nonnegative each, so c≥0 for all lags. Good.

Perturb function:
```
def binary_perturb(h,n,rng,fraction=0.10):
  h=h.copy()
  ones=np.nonzero(h)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(1-h)[0]
  m = max(1, int(n*fraction*0.5? number swaps))
  # perform m random swaps: choose random one, zero distinct.
  used? for _ in range(m): i=ones[rng.randint(len)]; j=zeros[rng.randint(len)]; if i!=j: swap.
  return h
```

       if f < bestf: bestx=x,bestc=t
    h[i]=bestx

  After sweep, update best c5 = max(c)*dx.
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  # maybe if elapsed break.

Let's refine continuous_coordinate_polish to be correct and efficient:
```
def coord_polish(h,n,deadline,pen=1e4):
    dx=2/n; target=0.5*n; off=n-1; L=2*n-1
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=h.copy(); bestC=compute_c5(best,n); c=None
    while time.time()<deadline:
        sweep_deadline = min(deadline, time.time()+3.0) # small sweep? Actually one sweep may take time depending. Use time checks inside.
        c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)
        curC=float(c.max())*dx
        if curC<bestC: bestC=curC; best=h.copy()
        # one coordinate sweep
        for i in range(n):
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            old=float(h[i])
            # Build B
            B=np.zeros(L)
            B[off + i - np.arange(n)] += 1.0 - h
            B[off + np.arange(n) - i] -= h
            c0_old=float(c[off])
            # candidate generation:
            cand=[0.0,1.0,0.5,old]
            if old>0.05: cand.append(old-0.1)
            if old<0.95: cand.append(old+0.1)
            if old>0.2: cand.append(old-0.25)
            if old<0.8: cand.append(old+0.25)
            # clip
            bestf=np.inf; bestx=old; bestc=None
            for x in cand:
                x=float(min(1,max(0,x)))
                if x==old and bestc is None:
                   bestc=c.copy(); bestf=float(c.max())+... continue?
                d=x-old
                t=c + B*d
                t[off]=c0_old - old*(1-old) + x*(1-x)
                sdev=float(h.sum() - target + d) # h.sum changes only current
                f=float(t.max()) + 0.5*pen*sdev*sdev
                if f<bestf:
                   bestf=f; bestx=x; bestc=t
            if bestc is None:
                bestc=c; bestx=old
            h[i]=bestx
            c=bestc
        # end for
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        h=project_feasible(h,n)
        curC=compute_c5(h,n)
        if curC<bestC: ...
    return best,bestC
```
Problem: `h.sum()` computed inside coordinate each candidate O(n), adding cost. We can maintain `sum_cur` globally: before sweep sum_cur=h.sum(); after each coordinate update sum_cur += bestx-old. Then sdev=sum_cur-target. Good. But project changes sum; update.
Also, `c=bestc` is a new array from candidate; if bestx=old maybe c old. Need if bestc from candidate with old? We'll include x=old candidate to initialize bestc.

But B computation uses `np.arange(n)` twice per coordinate; we can precompute `idx = np.arange(n)` top within function. Use local variable. `B[off + i - idx]` valid? off+i-idx ranges off+i-(n-1) to off+i, within 0..L? min off+i-(n-1)=0, max off+i ≤off+n-1=L-1. Good. `off+idx-i` min off-n+1=0, max off+n-1=L-1. Good.

When updating c with B*d, for d=0 t[off] overwritten. For other lags correct if only one variable changed and d≠0 bilinear linear. However note if k equals 0? handled. Good.

But if h values fractional and d nonzero, terms where both factors are i? only d=0; no other product involving h_i twice. So linear correct.

`build_D` concise:
```
def build_D(h,n):
    L=2*n-1; off=n-1; D=np.zeros((n,L), dtype=np.int16)
    h=np.asarray(h)
    for d in range(-(n-1), n):
        idx=off+d
        if d>=0:
            D[:n-d, idx] += h[d:]
            D[d:, idx] -= 1.0 - h[:n-d]
        else:
            e=-d
            D[e:, idx] += h[:n-e]
            D[:n-e, idx] -= 1.0 - h[e:]
    return D
```
For binary h int/float, D int16. Values when h fractional not used. If h int8? We'll use h float 0/1; D int16 from adding float? It will cast; okay. Need assign `- (1.0 - h)` to int16; if h float, values -1,0. Use h=np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64). For speed, can use int8 h? But top_n returns float. fine.

Potential bug: For d>=0, D[d:, idx] corresponds p=d..n-1; b=p-d = 0..n-1-d; we subtract 1-h[0:n-d]. Good. For d=0, D[:n,off]+=h; D[:n,off]-=1-h => 2h-1. For fractional, D0=2h-1. Good.

def pair_chunk_newmax(D, c, pi, pj, off):
    m=pi.size; L=c.size
    dd = D[pi] - D[pj]
    dd[:, off] -= 2
    ar = np.arange(m)
    dd[ar, off + pi - pj] += 1
    dd[ar, off + pj - pi] += 1
    return (c + dd).max(axis=1)
```

def binary_perturb(h,n,rng,fraction=0.03):
    h=h.copy()
    ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
    m=max(2,int(n*fraction*0.5)) # number swaps
    for _ in range(m):
        i=int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)]); j=int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
        if i!=j: h[i]=0;h[j]=1; update arrays? small m, just nonzero after.
    return top_n_binary? We know sum preserved.
```

def make_seeds(n, parent, parent_n, rng):
  seeds=[]
  if parent is not None:
     x0=np.linspace(0,2,parent_n); x1=np.linspace(0,2,n)
     hp=np.interp(x1,x0,parent)
     seeds.append(hp)
     # noise
     seeds.append(hp + 0.05*rng.randn(n))
  else:
     base=rng.rand(n)*0.1+0.4? top threshold random.
  # random
  arr=rng.rand(n); seeds.append(arr)
  # comb
  run=max(4,n//16); arr=((np.arange(n)//run)%2==0).astype(float); maybe scale with noise? top.
  # sin
  arr=np.sin(2*np.pi*np.arange(n)/n); seeds.append(arr)
  return seeds

def top_n_binary(a,n):
  a=np.asarray(a).ravel()
  if a.size<n: pad zeros; elif >n truncate
  target=n//2
  idx=np.argpartition(-a,target)[:target]? Better argsort stable for ties deterministic. n<=1000, argsort fine.
  hb=zeros; hb[np.argsort(-a)[:target]]=1; return hb
```

```
def topk_obj(x,n,q,pen):
  h=np.clip(np.asarray(x).ravel(),0,1)
  dx=2/n
  v=1-h
  c=np.correlate(h,v,'full')*dx
  m=min(q,c.size)
  if m<=0: return 0,...
  idx=np.argpartition(c, c.size-m)[c.size-m:]
  P=np.zeros(c.size); P[idx]=1.0/m
  F=float(c[idx].sum()/m)  # mean top
  R=P[::-1]
  A=np.convolve(R,v,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
  B=np.convolve(P,h,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
  g=dx*(A-B)
  dev=h.sum()-0.5*n
  if pen:
    F+=0.5*pen*dev*dev; g=g+pen*dev
  return F,g
```
Is F mean top or sum top? Mean top might ignore overall scaling. Sum top maybe. Use mean top plus max? We want minimize max; mean top with q small closely approximates max. For q=1, mean top = max. For q=5 average top5. Good. Maybe add 0.5*max? F = 0.5*c[idx].max() + 0.5*c[idx].mean() to focus both. But gradient weights: need weights 0.5/m for top plus 0.5 for max. Could use LSE easier. But topq mean gradient okay.

For q=1, `argpartition` with kth? If m=1, `idx=np.argpartition(c, c.size-1)[c.size-1:]` returns max index? For partition, kth for last. Better if m==1: idx=[int(c.argmax())]. General.

Let's specify binary_local_search robust:
```
def binary_local_search(h0,n,deadline,max_time,seed=42):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=top_n_binary(h0,n)
    c=np.array(np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full'), dtype=np.int32)
    M=int(c.max())
    bestM=M; besth=h.copy()
    D=build_D(h,n)
    stop=min(deadline, time.time()+max_time)
    stalls=0
    perturbs=0
    while time.time() < stop:
        ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
        if ones.size==0 or zeros.size==0: break
        zn=zeros.size; total=ones.size*zn
        if total==0: break
        pi=np.repeat(ones, zn); pj=np.tile(zeros, ones.size)
        chunk=1024 if n<=256 else 512
        best_nm=M; best_pos=-1
        rstart=rng.randint(0,total)
        arange_chunk=np.arange(chunk)
        for start in range(0,total,chunk):
            idx=(rstart+arange_chunk[:min(chunk,total-start)])%total
            nm=pair_newmax(D,c,pi[idx],pj[idx],n-1)
            mn=int(nm.min()); loc=int(np.argmin(nm))
            if mn<best_nm:
                best_nm=mn; best_pos=int(idx[loc])
                if best_nm==M-1:
                    break
        if best_pos>=0 and best_nm<M:
            p=best_pos; i=int(pi[p]); j=int(pj[p])
            delta=D[i]-D[j]
            delta=delta.astype(np.int32,copy=True)
            delta[n-1]-=2
            delta[n-1+i-j]+=1
            delta[n-1+j-i]+=1
            c+=delta
            h[i]=0; h[j]=1
            D=build_D(h,n)
            M=best_nm
            if M<bestM:
               bestM=M; besth=h.copy(); stalls=0
            else: stalls=0 # bestM only M? no, M is current, bestM if lower. If M<bestM update; else current M maybe same? If best_nm<M old, then M<oldM but could >bestM if bestM from previous? no if current continuous descends, M always <= previous current, bestM min. So update.
        else:
            stalls+=1
            if stalls>=2 and perturbs<6 and time.time()+5<stop:
                h=besth.copy()
                ones0=np.flatnonzero(h==1); zeros0=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
                msw=max(1,int(n*0.03))
                for _ in range(msw):
                    if ones0.size==0 or zeros0.size==0: break
                    ia=int(ones0[rng.randint(ones0.size)]); jb=int(zeros0[rng.randint(zeros0.size)])
                    if ia!=jb:
                       h[ia]=0; h[jb]=1
                       ones0=np.flatnonzero(h==1); zeros0=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
                c=np.array(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'), dtype=np.int32)
                D=build_D(h,n)
                M=int(c.max())
                if M<bestM: bestM=M; besth=h.copy()
                stalls=0; perturbs+=1
            elif stalls>=6:
                break
    return besth,bestM
```
One issue: `if best_nm==M-1: break` after chunk; then in apply, `best_pos` is idx[loc]. Good. But early break may stop before full scan; okay.
Another: If `best_nm<M` but `best_pos` maybe from a chunk where M changed? M constant during scan. Good.
After applying, M=best_nm, but could be lower than c.max() due to error? To be safe set M=int(c.max()). But for speed use best_nm; maybe verify occasionally? Could do `M=int(c.max())` anyway O(L) cheap. Let's set M=int(c.max()) after update to ensure consistency.
After perturb, recompute c/D/M.

`stalls` logic: If current M not improved over previous but bestM maybe lower? Actually if no improving swap from current M, current M == bestM? Since we always update best when M decreases. Yes. If stuck, perturb. Limit perturbs 8.

```
def top_idx(c,m):
    m=int(min(m,c.size))
    if m<=0: return np.empty(0,int)
    if m==1: return c.argmax()
    k=c.size-m
    return np.argpartition(c,k)[k:]
```

`blend_obj`:
```
def blend_obj(x,n,m1,m2,alpha,pen):
  h=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel(),0,1)
  if h.size!=n: ...
  v=1-h
  dx=2.0/n
  c=np.correlate(h,v,'full')*dx
  c=np.maximum(c,0)
  L=c.size
  P=np.zeros(L)
  F=0.0
  if m1>0:
    idx=top_idx(c,m1); F += float(c[idx].mean()); P[idx] += 1.0/idx.size
  if m2>0:
    idx2=top_idx(c,m2); F += alpha*float(c[idx2].mean()); P[idx2] += alpha/idx2.size
  R=P[::-1]
  A=np.convolve(R,v,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
  B=np.convolve(P,h,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
  g=dx*(A-B)
  dev=float(h.sum()-0.5*n)
  if pen!=0:
    F+=0.5*pen*dev*dev
    g = g + pen*dev
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=zeros
  return float(F),g
```
If idx size zero? c size >0. `idx.size`. Good.

`polish_blend(h0,n,deadline,stage_time)`:
```
def polish_blend(h0,n,deadline,stage_time):
  work=project_feasible(h0,n)
  best=work.copy(); bc=compute_c5(best,n)
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  stages=[(1,10,0.3),(3,30,0.5),(5,100,1.0),(10,300,1.5),(1,50,0.5),(3,200,2.0)]
  for m1,m2,alpha in stages:
    if time.time()>deadline-5: break
    stage_dl=min(deadline-2,time.time()+max(2,stage_time))
    _stop_time=stage_dl
    try:
       res=minimize(blend_obj,work,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,args=(n,m1,m2,alpha,1e4),callback=_stop_cb,options={'maxiter':1000000,'maxls':30,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-8})
       x=res.x if res is not None else work
    except Exception: x=work
    work=project_feasible(x,n)
    cw=compute_c5(work,n)
    if cw<bc: bc=cw; best=work.copy()
    for cand in candidate_variants(work,n):
       cc=compute_c5(cand,n)
       if cc<bc: bc=cc; best=cand.copy()
    if bc < cw - 1e-12:
       work=best.copy()
  return best,bc
```
Bounds list length n each call; okay. `stage_time` maybe 30-60.

Candidate variants: If work binary, 1-h[::-1] sum? project.

Now, seed function. We need use parent global but not copy entries. `np.array(initial_h_values)` okay? It creates copy but not literal. Good.
```
def downsample_parent(parent,parent_n,n):
    if parent is None: return None
    if parent_n==n: return parent
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,parent_n)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    return np.interp(x1,x0,parent)
```
Seeds:
```
def make_seeds(n,parent,seed_parent,rng):
   ps=[]
   base = downsample_parent(parent,seed_parent,n)
   if base is None:
      base=0.45+0.1*rng.rand(n)
      ps.append(base)
   else:
      ps.append(base)
      ps.append(base + 0.05*rng.randn(n))
   arr=rng.rand(n)*0.8+0.1
   ps.append(arr)
   run_len=max(3,n//12)
   arr=((np.arange(n)//run_len)%2).astype(float)*0.8+0.1
   ps.append(arr)
   arr=np.sin(2*np.pi*(np.arange(n)%? maybe linspace)?)
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n); arr=np.sin(np.pi*x)*0.5+0.5
   ps.append(arr)
   return ps[:4]
```

`project_feasible`: We need ensure exact sum. Let's rewrite concise and robust:
```
def project_feasible(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size!=n:
       a2=np.zeros(n); m=min(a.size,n); a2[:m]=a[:m]; a=a2
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.5)
    target=0.5*n
    lo=float(a.min())-1; hi=float(a.max())+1
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(a-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(a-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # adjust diff
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; s=room.sum()
          if s<=1e-15: break
          h += diff*room/s
       else:
          s=h.sum()
          if s<=1e-15: break
          h += diff*h/s
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
If a exact binary, this may still shift by mid not threshold? Let's test: a binary sum target. Bisection finds shift mid such that sum clip(a-mid)=target. There may be plateau, bisection might converge to some mid not equal to threshold; h fractional? Suppose all 0/1, target n/2. For mid between 0 and1, sum = #ones with 1-mid = 200*(1-mid). To equal 200, mid=0. So bisection will push lo? If s>target? For mid small, s>200? mid=0.25 ->150<200 => hi=mid. It converges mid=0, h=a. Then diff 0. Good. If mid=0, hi/lo mid 0. okay. Might produce exact binary. Good.

`candidate_variants`: for continuous, `0.5*(h + h[::-1])` maybe sum okay. `1.0 - h[::-1]` sum maybe n - sum reversed = n - n/2 = n/2, exact; but project safe.
```
def candidate_variants(h,n):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   xs=[h, h[::-1], 1.0-h[::-1], 0.5*(h+h[::-1])]
   return [project_feasible(x,n) for x in xs]
```

    out.append(project_feasible(h[::-1],n))
    out.append(project_feasible(1.0-h[::-1],n))

`make_seeds`:
```
def make_seeds(n,parent,parent_n,seed_base):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed_base*1000+1)
    seeds=[]
    base=None
    if parent is not None and parent_n>0:
       if parent_n==n: base=parent.copy()
       else:
          base=np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n), np.linspace(0,2,parent_n), parent)
       seeds.append(base)
       seeds.append(base + 0.05*rng.randn(n))
    else:
       base=0.45+0.15*rng.rand(n)
       seeds.append(base)
    seeds.append(0.2+0.6*rng.rand(n))
    run_len=max(4,n//12)
    x=(np.arange(n)//run_len)%2
    seeds.append(0.25+0.5*x)
    x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
    seeds.append(0.5+0.25*np.sin(np.pi*x))
    return seeds[:5]
```
Seed values in [0,1], top threshold okay.

  ns=[]

  # sort by n? maybe prioritize 200,300,240,400,360,parent
  order={200:0,300:1,240:2,400:3,360:4}

`project_feasible` shorter:
```
def project_feasible(a,n):
 a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
 if a.size!=n:
  b=np.zeros(n); m=min(n,a.size); b[:m]=a[:m]; a=b
 a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.5); target=0.5*n
 lo=float(a.min())-1; hi=float(a.max())+1
 for _ in range(60):
  mid=0.5*(lo+hi); s=np.clip(a-mid,0,1).sum()
  if s>target: lo=mid
  else: hi=mid
 h=np.clip(a-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
 for _ in range(30):
  d=target-h.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-13: break
  if d>0:
   r=1-h; s=r.sum(); h += d*r/s if s>1e-15 else 0
  else:
   s=h.sum(); h += d*h/s if s>1e-15 else 0
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 d=target-h.sum()
 if abs(d)>1e-12:
  if d>0: h[int(np.argmax(1-h))]=min(1,h[int(np.argmax(1-h))]+d)
  else: h[int(np.argmax(h))]=max(0,h[int(np.argmax(h))]+d)
 return h
```

`top_n_binary` compact:

def top_n_binary(a,n):
 a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
 if a.size<n: a=np.concatenate([a,np.zeros(n-a.size)])
 elif a.size>n: a=a[:n]
 if np.all((a==0)|(a==1)) and abs(a.sum()-0.5*n)<1e-12: return a
 idx=np.argsort(-a)[:n//2]
 b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1; return b
```

Local search:
```
def binary_local_search(h0,n,deadline,seed=42):
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed); h=top_n_binary(h0,n)
 c=...; M=...; bestM=M; besth=h.copy(); D=build_D(h,n); off=n-1; stalls=0; perturbs=0
 while time.time()<deadline:
  ones=... zeros=...
  if ... break
  pi=np.repeat(ones, zeros.size); pj=np.tile(zeros, ones.size); total=pi.size
  chunk=1024 if n<=256 else 512
  bestnm=M; bestpos=-1
  order=(np.arange(total)+rng.randint(total))%total
  for s in range(0,total,chunk):
    idx=order[s:s+chunk]
    nm=pair_newmax(D,c,pi[idx],pj[idx],off)
    mn=int(nm.min()); loc=int(np.argmin(nm))
    if mn<bestnm:
      bestnm=mn; bestpos=int(idx[loc])
      if bestnm==M-1: break
  if bestpos>=0 and bestnm<M:
    p=bestpos; i=int(pi[p]); j=int(pj[p])
    delta=(D[i]-D[j]).astype(np.int32)
    delta[off]-=2; delta[off+i-j]+=1; delta[off+j-i]+=1
    c+=delta; h[i]=0; h[j]=1; D=build_D(h,n)
    M=int(c.max())
    if M<bestM: bestM=M; besth=h.copy()
  else:
    stalls+=1
    if stalls>=2 and perturbs<8 and time.time()+5<deadline:
      h=besth.copy(); ones=... zeros=...
      for _ in range(max(1,int(n*0.03))):
       if not ones.size or not zeros.size: break
       ia=int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)]); jb=int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
       if ia!=jb:
         h[ia]=0; h[jb]=1
         ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
      c=np.array(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'),dtype=np.int32); D=build_D(h,n); M=int(c.max())
      if M<bestM: bestM=M; besth=h.copy()
      stalls=0; perturbs+=1
    elif stalls>=6: break
 return besth,bestM
```
This is ~45 lines. Good. Note `rng.randint(total)` if total=0 impossible. If total=1, `rng.randint(1)` returns 0; okay.

def blend_obj(x,n,m1,m2,alpha,pen):
 h=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
 if h.size!=n: ... maybe skip for brevity? L-BFGS size constant. Could omit to save; but safe? If bug, fail. Keep small:

 h=np.clip(h,0,1); v=1-h; dx=2/n
 c=np.correlate(h,v,'full')*dx; c=np.maximum(c,0.0); L=c.size; P=np.zeros(L); F=0.0
 if m1>0:
  idx=top_idx(c,m1); F+=c[idx].mean(); P[idx]+=1.0/idx.size
 if m2>0:
  idx=top_idx(c,m2); F+=alpha*c[idx].mean(); P[idx]+=alpha/idx.size
 F=float(F)
 A=np.convolve(P,v,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
 B=np.convolve(P[::-1],h,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
 g=dx*(A-B)
 d=float(h.sum()-0.5*n)
 if pen:
  F+=0.5*pen*d*d; g=g+pen*d
 if not np.isfinite(F): F=1e100; g=np.zeros(n)
 return F,g
```

def make_seeds(n,parent,parent_n,seed):

Potential problem: `minimize` with `bounds` list length n, `jac=True`; if objective function returns g shape n. Good.

Potential problem: L-BFGS-B with non-smooth top idx might fail, catch.

Potential problem: In `blend_obj`, c has length 2n-1; top_idx uses argpartition modifying view? no.

Potential problem: In binary local search, `c.max()` may be numpy int; cast. Good.

```

Let's double-check corrected gradient with P weights and F mean top. If P sum not 1, F and gradient scale accordingly. For m1=1, P sum for max part=1; F max. If m2 α=0.5 mean top10, P sum α*10*(0.1?) actually P adds α/10 on 10 -> sum α. F term α*mean. Good.

If P all zeros (unlikely), A,B zero, gradient only penalty. okay.

Now final code. Need ensure indentation and syntax. Let's draft final. (In final only code block.)

Let's test small n mental for binary local search: n=4 h=1010. c? D local. It will try swaps. likely.

Potential bug in `pair_newmax` with `dd[ar, off + pi - pj] += 1` if multiple rows have same position; advanced indexing with array indices assigns one per row. Good. If `off + pi - pj` equals `off` (i=j), not. `dd[:, off] -=2` before additions, okay. If p1/p2 same? no.

Potential bug: In `binary_local_search`, after first improved swap, `D=build_D(h,n)`. But `pi,pj` arrays built from old ones/zeros; next loop rebuild. Good.

Potential bug: In local search scan, if early break at M-1, there may be a pair in same chunk with even smaller M-2 that appears later in chunk; we only take chunk min `nm.min()`. Potential bug: In final if `best_h` exact binary but sum target, we check `np.all(h>=-1e-12) and abs(h.sum()...)`; we don't check binary values. We clip and return even if fractional exact sum. That's okay; project not needed. If fractional but sum exact, objective evaluated. Good.

Potential bug: `compute_c5` for h length mismatch? In final length correct. In `compute_c5` we use n parameter; if h is longer? It will correlate h and 1-h with length h.size, but dx uses n. `compute_c5`:
```

if h.size<n: h=np.concatenate([h,np.zeros(n-h.size)])
elif h.size>n: h=h[:n]
```
This makes robust.

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_stop_time = 0.0

def _stop_cb(x, *args):
    return time.time() > _stop_time

def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size < n:
        h = np.concatenate([h, np.zeros(n - h.size)])
    elif h.size > n:
        h = h[:n]
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx
    return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0

def project_feasible(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        b = np.zeros(n)
        m = min(n, a.size)
        b[:m] = a[:m]
        a = b
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.5)
    target = 0.5 * n
    lo = float(np.min(a)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(a)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(a - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(a - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        d = target - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            h += d * room / s if s > 1e-15 else 0.0
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            h += d * h / s if s > 1e-15 else 0.0
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - h.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-12:
        if d > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + d)
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] + d)
    return h

def top_n_binary(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size < n:
        a = np.concatenate([a, np.zeros(n - a.size)])
    elif a.size > n:
        a = a[:n]
    if np.all((a == 0.0) | (a == 1.0)) and abs(a.sum() - 0.5 * n) < 1e-12:
        return a
    idx = np.argsort(-a)[:n // 2]
    b = np.zeros(n)
    b[idx] = 1.0
    return b

def build_D(h, n):
    hb = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int8).ravel()
    if hb.size < n:
        hb = np.concatenate([hb, np.zeros(n - hb.size, dtype=np.int8)])
    elif hb.size > n:
        hb = hb[:n]
    L = 2 * n - 1
    off = n - 1
    D = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=np.int16)
    for d in range(-(n - 1), n):
        idx = off + d
        if d >= 0:
            D[:n - d, idx] += hb[d:]
            D[d:, idx] -= 1 - hb[:n - d]
        else:
            e = -d
            D[e:, idx] += hb[:n - e]
            D[:n - e, idx] -= 1 - hb[e:]
    return D

def pair_newmax(D, c, pi, pj, off):
    m = pi.size
    dd = D[pi] - D[pj]
    dd[:, off] -= 2
    ar = np.arange(m)
    dd[ar, off + pi - pj] += 1
    dd[ar, off + pj - pi] += 1
    return (c + dd).max(axis=1)

def binary_local_search(h0, n, deadline, seed=42):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = top_n_binary(h0, n)
    c = np.array(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full"), dtype=np.int32)
    M = int(c.max())
    bestM = M
    besth = h.copy()
    D = build_D(h, n)
    off = n - 1
    stalls = 0
    perturbs = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1.0)
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0.0)
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break
        pi = np.repeat(ones, zeros.size)
        pj = np.tile(zeros, ones.size)
        total = pi.size
        if total == 0:
            break
        chunk = 1024 if n <= 256 else 512
        bestnm = M
        bestpos = -1
        order = (np.arange(total) + rng.randint(total)) % total
        for s in range(0, total, chunk):
            idx = order[s:s + chunk]
            nm = pair_newmax(D, c, pi[idx], pj[idx], off)
            mn = int(nm.min())
            if mn < bestnm:
                bestnm = mn
                bestpos = int(idx[int(np.argmin(nm))])
                if bestnm == M - 1:
                    break
        if bestpos >= 0 and bestnm < M:
            p = bestpos
            i = int(pi[p])
            j = int(pj[p])
            delta = (D[i] - D[j]).astype(np.int32)
            delta[off] -= 2
            delta[off + i - j] += 1
            delta[off + j - i] += 1
            c += delta
            h[i] = 0.0
            h[j] = 1.0
            D = build_D(h, n)
            M = int(c.max())
            if M < bestM:
                bestM = M
                besth = h.copy()
        else:
            stalls += 1
            if stalls >= 2 and perturbs < 8 and time.time() + 5.0 < deadline:
                h = besth.copy()
                ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1.0)
                zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0.0)
                for _ in range(max(1, int(0.03 * n))):
                    if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
                        break
                    ia = int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
                    jb = int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
                    if ia != jb:
                        h[ia] = 0.0
                        h[jb] = 1.0
                        ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1.0)
                        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0.0)
                c = np.array(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full"), dtype=np.int32)
                D = build_D(h, n)
                M = int(c.max())
                if M < bestM:
                    bestM = M
                    besth = h.copy()
                stalls = 0
                perturbs += 1
            elif stalls >= 6:
                break
    return besth, bestM

def top_idx(c, m):
    m = int(min(m, c.size))
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if m == 1:
        return np.array([int(c.argmax())], dtype=int)
    k = c.size - m
    return np.argpartition(c, k)[k:]

def blend_obj(x, n, m1, m2, alpha, pen):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size < n:
        h = np.concatenate([h, np.zeros(n - h.size)])
    elif h.size > n:
        h = h[:n]
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    v = 1.0 - h
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, v, mode="full") * dx
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    L = c.size
    P = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    F = 0.0
    if m1 > 0:
        idx = top_idx(c, m1)
        F += float(c[idx].mean())
        P[idx] += 1.0 / idx.size
    if m2 > 0:
        idx = top_idx(c, m2)
        F += alpha * float(c[idx].mean())
        P[idx] += alpha / idx.size
    A = np.convolve(P, v, mode="full")[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    B = np.convolve(P[::-1], h, mode="full")[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = dx * (A - B)
    dev = float(h.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    if pen != 0.0:
        F += 0.5 * pen * dev * dev
        g = g + pen * dev
    if not (np.isfinite(F) and np.all(np.isfinite(g))):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    return float(F), g

def polish_blend(h0, n, deadline, stage_time):
    global _stop_time
    work = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best = work.copy()
    bc = compute_c5(best, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    stages = [(1, 10, 0.5), (3, 30, 0.8), (5, 80, 1.0), (10, 300, 1.2), (1, 50, 0.4)]
    for m1, m2, al in stages:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        _stop_time = min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + float(stage_time))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                blend_obj, work, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                args=(n, int(m1), int(m2), float(al), 1e3),
                callback=_stop_cb,
                options={"maxiter": 1000000, "maxls": 30, "ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": 1e-8}
            )
            x = res.x if res is not None else work
        except Exception:
            x = work
        work = project_feasible(x, n)
        cw = compute_c5(work, n)
        if np.isfinite(cw) and cw < bc:
            bc = cw
            best = work.copy()
        for cand in (work, work[::-1], 1.0 - work[::-1], 0.5 * (work + work[::-1])):
            cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
            cc = compute_c5(cand, n)
            if cc < bc:
                bc = cc
                best = cand.copy()
        if bc < cw - 1e-12:
            work = best.copy()
    return best, bc

def make_seeds(n, parent, parent_n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    seeds = []
    if parent is not None and parent_n > 0:
        if parent_n == n:
            base = parent.copy()
        else:
            base = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n), np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, parent_n), parent)
        seeds.append(base)
        seeds.append(base + 0.05 * rng.randn(n))
    else:
        seeds.append(0.45 + 0.15 * rng.rand(n))
    seeds.append(0.2 + 0.6 * rng.rand(n))
    rl = max(4, n // 12)
    seeds.append(0.25 + 0.5 * ((np.arange(n) // rl) % 2).astype(float))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    seeds.append(0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(np.pi * x))
    return seeds[:5]

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1100.0
    end = t0 + max(20.0, total_budget - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        parent_n = int(parent.size)
        if parent_n < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
            raise ValueError("bad parent")
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        parent_n = 0

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e9
    best_n = 0

    if parent is not None:
        h = project_feasible(parent, parent_n)
        c = compute_c5(h, parent_n)
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_c = c
        best_n = parent_n
        for cand in (h[::-1], 1.0 - h[::-1], 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])):
            cand = project_feasible(cand, parent_n)
            cc = compute_c5(cand, parent_n)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = cand.copy()
                best_n = parent_n

    reserve = max(15.0, min((end - t0) * 0.35, 350.0))
    discrete_end = max(t0 + 5.0, end - reserve)

    base_ns = [200, 240, 300, 360, 400]
    if parent_n % 2 == 0 and 80 <= parent_n <= 600:
        base_ns.append(parent_n)
    ns = []
    for nn in base_ns:
        if nn % 2 == 0 and 80 <= nn <= 600 and nn not in ns:
            ns.append(nn)
    ns.sort()

    fracs = {200: 0.30, 240: 0.15, 300: 0.25, 360: 0.12, 400: 0.18}
    fr_sum = 0.0
    for nn in ns:
        fr_sum += fracs.get(nn, 0.05)
    if fr_sum <= 0.0:
        fr_sum = 1.0

    for nn in ns:
        if time.time() >= discrete_end:
            break
        alloc = max(10.0, (discrete_end - time.time()) * fracs.get(nn, 0.05) / fr_sum)
        phase_dl = min(discrete_end, time.time() + alloc)
        seeds = make_seeds(nn, parent, parent_n, seed + int(nn))
        best_seed_h = None
        best_seed_M = 10 ** 9
        sel_t = max(3.0, min(10.0, alloc * 0.12))
        for k, s0 in enumerate(seeds[:3]):
            if time.time() >= phase_dl - 1.0:
                break
            dl = time.time() + min(sel_t, phase_dl - time.time())
            h1, M1 = binary_local_search(s0, nn, dl, seed + nn * 7 + k)
            if M1 < best_seed_M:
                best_seed_M = M1
                best_seed_h = h1
            cc = 2.0 * float(M1) / float(nn)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = h1.copy()
                best_n = nn
            if best_c < 0.3800:
                break
        if best_seed_h is not None and time.time() + 2.0 < phase_dl:
            h1, M1 = binary_local_search(best_seed_h, nn, phase_dl, seed + nn * 13 + 7)
            cc = 2.0 * float(M1) / float(nn)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = h1.copy()
                best_n = nn
        if best_c < 0.3800:
            break

    polish_dl = end - 5.0
    inits = []
    if best_h is not None:
        inits.append((best_h, best_n))
    if parent is not None and parent_n > 0:
        inits.append((parent, parent_n))
    done = set()
    for hh, nn in inits:
        if time.time() > polish_dl - 15.0:
            break
        if nn in done:
            continue
        done.add(nn)
        st = max(5.0, min(60.0, (polish_dl - time.time()) * 0.40))
        ph, pc = polish_blend(hh, nn, polish_dl, st)
        if pc < best_c:
            best_c = pc
            best_h = ph.copy()
            best_n = nn

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 200
        best_h = project_feasible(0.45 + 0.15 * rng.rand(best_n), best_n)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    else:
        h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).ravel()
        n = int(best_n)
        target = 0.5 * n
        ok = (h.size == n and np.all(h >= -1e-12) and np.all(h <= 1.0 + 1e-12)
              and abs(h.sum() - target) < 1e-10)
        if not ok:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
        else:
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
        best_h = h

    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
